The next weight could not be checked."; "Your choice is saved; the next card could not be checked."; "Your saved workout changed since this offer. Check again for a current one."; "No new weight to agree to yet. Your saved sets are kept."; "Saved. The new weight applies on a later workout." (:160-165);
  - the offer headings "<lift name>: next weight" and "<lift name>: set your working weight" [Q11, Q12] (:281);
  - "Yes" / "Not now" [Q8, Q9] (:166-167, the offer card's buttons);
  - "Set <n>: <w> lb" [Q10] (:283, with today-bindings.mjs:697's unit);
  - "Offered weight for each set" [Q13] (:282, aria-label);
  - the earn explanation with its four reasons [Q14-Q18]: "<lift name>: you topped the rep window at <loads> (workouts on <dates>). Offer: <loads>, <increase>. Nothing changes unless you say yes; it then applies on a later <lift name> workout." (FC01 :159-160), <increase> = "a one-step increase after topping the rep window" (:158), "a two-step increase, because your last set had <n> reps left" (:155), "an early increase from one top of the window, with <n> reps left on your last set" (:157), "an early increase from one top of the window: your opening set was hard, your last set had <n> reps left" (:156);
  - the two adopt explanations [Q19, Q20]: "<lift name>: on <date> you completed every set at <loads> (the card said <loads>). Offer: make that your working weight. This sets your working weight; it is not an earned increase. Nothing changes unless you say yes." (:168-170) and "<lift name>: on <date> you completed every set at <loads>, and no working weight was on file. Offer: make that your working weight. This sets your working weight; it is not an earned increase. Nothing changes unless you say yes." (:169-170); their shared frame is also the frame of the D:804 sentence (coupling, 2.3 B);
  - the undo explanation [Q21]: "<lift name>: undo the choice you agreed to before any workout used it. Your working weight goes back to <loads>. The workouts it came from stay recorded and are not counted again." (:407);
  - the load formats [Q22]: "<w> lb on every set", "<a> lb, <b> lb, <c> lb" per set, "no load" for a set without a weight (:148-150).
